===== MAKE IOS =====
[make exit=0]
--- last 25 lines of make-ios.out ---
touch /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app
rsync -a --delete \
	  --exclude='*.elc' --exclude='.git*' --exclude='Makefile*' \
	  /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/../lisp/ /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/lisp/
## subdirs.el is generated by the normal build, not stored in
## the source tree, so the rsync above never ships it -- and
## without it startup.el's normal-top-level never extends
## load-path into lisp/mail, lisp/net, lisp/emacs-lisp, etc.
## Everything not preloaded then fails with "Cannot open load
## file" (first observed on-device as rfc6068 breaking
## package.el).  Generate the standard one-liner form.
printf '%s\n%s\n' \
	  ';; -*- no-byte-compile: t -*-' \
	  "(if (fboundp 'normal-top-level-add-subdirs-to-load-path) (normal-top-level-add-subdirs-to-load-path))" \
	  > /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/lisp/subdirs.el
touch /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/lisp/loadup.el
rsync -a --delete \
	  --exclude='.git*' --exclude='Makefile*' --exclude='emacs.1' \
	  /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/../etc/ /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/etc/
touch /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/etc/charsets || true
cp -f /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/cross/src/emacs /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/emacs
ios/Makefile: no AppIcon.png; skipping asset catalog
  (App Store submissions require one; see ios/README)
ios/Makefile: CODESIGN_IDENTITY unset; producing
  unsigned bundle suitable for the Simulator only.
--- make errors (if any) ---
